Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any post-quantum module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 686. The projective resolution has length at most 801 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 686 + O(1/n^801). Summing over all modes and applying the post-quantum convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)
